1799 — two handed hang back stretch (with training wall bars)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1799 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: hanging.

Hanging from wall bars with both hands lets gravity stretch your lats and decompress your back. Great after heavy pulling days.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hang from the source-defined overhead support; do not invent a new bar, anchor height or grip orientation.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 1799.json#/description

Arms Hands

Grip a wall bar overhead with both hands and walk your feet out slightly.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1799.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Grip a wall bar overhead with both hands and walk your feet out slightly.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1799.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Stall/training wall bars, Wall surface. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1799.json#/description

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Grip a wall bar overhead with both hands and walk your feet out slightly. 1799.json#/instructions/0
  2. Let your body hang and sink your hips back, relaxing your upper back and shoulders. 1799.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. 1799.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds without bouncing. 1799.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release gently and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one-sided. 1799.json#/instructions/4

1800 — cable suspension back stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1800 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Holding a cable attachment, you lean back and let your weight stretch your lats and back. Good for opening up the upper body.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: authored_grounded_default_for_unspecified_cable_band_or_suspension_posture. 1800.json#/equipment

Arms Hands

Hold the cable attachment or handles with both hands at about chest height.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1800.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 1800.json#/equipment

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1800.json#/equipment

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hold the cable attachment or handles with both hands at about chest height. 1800.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lean back with straight arms and sit your hips back until you feel your back lengthen. 1800.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. 1800.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds without bouncing. 1800.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release gently and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one-sided. 1800.json#/instructions/4

  • Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. Step 3; 1800.json#/instructions/2

1801 — one arm lat stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1801 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Reaching one arm overhead and leaning to the side stretches a single lat. Good for finding tight spots on each side.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand tall and hold a pole or frame with one hand above head height.

  3. Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. 1801.json#/instructions/2

1802 — side lat stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1802 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Bending sideways with an arm overhead lengthens the lat and the side of your trunk. A quick stretch for the flanks.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

1803 — sitting lower trunk extensor stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1803 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Seated and folding forward, you stretch the muscles along your lower back. A gentle way to ease lower back tightness.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the floor or a bench with your knees slightly bent and your feet flat.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1803.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1803.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the floor or a bench with your knees slightly bent and your feet flat. 1803.json#/instructions/0
  2. Round your back and fold your chest toward your thighs, letting your arms hang down. 1803.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. 1803.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds without bouncing. 1803.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release gently and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one-sided. 1803.json#/instructions/4

1804 — seated lower trunk extensor lateral flexor stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1804 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Seated, you fold forward and sideways to stretch both the lower back and the side of your trunk. It targets one side at a time.

1806 — sitting side reach stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1806 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Seated, reaching over to the side stretches your lats and obliques. It is easy on the hips and good for desk breaks.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit tall with your legs crossed or your feet flat and place one hand on the floor beside you.

Complete ordered source instructions

  3. Keep your shoulders relaxed and your breathing slow and steady. 1806.json#/instructions/2

Complete ordered source instructions

1901 — kneeling face down adductor stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1901 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

A floor stretch that opens up the inner thighs while you rest face down on your forearms.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Kneel on a mat, then lower your chest and forearms to the floor.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1901.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the torso until the source's arm action starts; preserve documented load, handle or support contacts.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1901.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Kneel on a mat, then lower your chest and forearms to the floor. 1901.json#/instructions/0
  2. Slide your knees wide apart and sink your hips back toward your heels until you feel the inner thighs stretch. 1901.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your breathing slow and stay relaxed, without bouncing. 1901.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold for 20-30 seconds, feeling a gentle pull rather than pain. 1901.json#/instructions/3
  5. Release slowly and repeat, switching sides if the stretch is one sided. 1901.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the floor with your legs spread wide. 1902.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your knees pointing up. 1902.json#/instructions/1
  3. Hinge forward from the hips with a long spine. 1902.json#/instructions/2
  4. Walk your hands forward until you feel the stretch. 1902.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds, breathing slowly. 1902.json#/instructions/4
  6. Come up slowly. 1902.json#/instructions/5
